<script type="text/javascript">

    $jq = jQuery.noConflict();



    function check_fidelite_cart()

    {

        //alert('test');

        
        $jq.ajax({

            url: "https://www.leaderplant.com/ajax/check_fidelite.php",

            type : 'post',

            data: "id_customer=&id_cart=",

            success: function(data){

                if (data == "")

                    window.location='https://www.leaderplant.com/checkout/onepage/';

                else

                    alert(data);

            }

        });

        
    }

    $jq(function() {

        $jq( "#accordion" ).accordion();

        $jq('.bxslider').bxSlider({

            adaptativeHeight:true,

            slideWidth: 265,

            slideMargin: 0,

            minSlides: 2,

            maxSlides: 4,

            controls:true,

            pager:false

        });

        $jq('.bxslider_product_img').bxSlider({

            adaptativeHeight:true,

            slideWidth: 265,

            slideMargin: 0,

            minSlides: 1,

            maxSlides: 1,

            controls:true,

            pager:false

        });


        $jq('.bxsliderleft').bxSlider({

            auto : true,

            minSlides:4,

            adaptativeHeight:true,

            controls:false,

            pager:false

        });

        $jq('.bxsliderleft2').bxSlider({

            minSlides:4,

            adaptativeHeight:true,

            controls:false,

            pager:true

        });

        /** gestion des produits group?s turboplant et ecoplant **/


        /** **/

        /** Close PopUp **/

        $jq("#fermer").click(function() {

            $jq(".fancybox-overlay").trigger("click");

        });

        $jq("#fermer2").click(function() {

            $jq("#continuerAchat").trigger("click");

        });



        $jq('.linkComtpe').click(function(){

            var width  = $jq(window).width();

            if(width < 786){

                window.location.assign($jq(this).attr('data-href'));

                return false;

            }

        });

    });

    /*** Modif Menu ***/

    $jq('html').mouseleave(function() {

        $jq('.wp-custom-menu-popup').css('display','none');

        $jq('.menu').removeClass('active');

    });



    $jq( document ).ready(function() {

        var secondaryNav = $jq('.nav-container');

        var secondaryNavTopPosition = secondaryNav.offset().top;





        $jq(window).on('scroll', function(){



            if($jq(window).scrollTop() > secondaryNavTopPosition ) {

                secondaryNav.addClass('is-fixed');

                if($jq(window).scrollTop() > secondaryNavTopPosition+10 ) {

                    secondaryNav.addClass('is-fixed-transition');

                }

                setTimeout(function() {

                    // secondaryNav.addClass('animate-children');

                    // $jq('#cd-logo').addClass('slide-in');

                    $jq('.searchautocomplete').insertAfter('#custommenu');

                    $jq('.searchautocomplete').addClass('slide-in');

                }, 50);

            } else {

                secondaryNav.removeClass('is-fixed');

                secondaryNav.removeClass('is-fixed-transition');

                setTimeout(function() {

                    // secondaryNav.removeClass('animate-children');

                    // $jq('#cd-logo').removeClass('slide-in');

                    $jq('.searchautocomplete').removeClass('slide-in');

                    $jq('.searchautocomplete').insertAfter('.headerPhrase');

                }, 50);

            }

        });



        /*** CALL TO ACTION ***/

        $jq(".call_action_title").on('click', function(){

            var callContent = $jq(this).parent();

            if( callContent.hasClass('active') ){

                $jq(this).parent().removeClass('active');

            }else{

                $jq('.call_action_block').removeClass('active');

                $jq(this).parent().addClass('active');

            }

        });

        $jq('.call_action_content-close').on('click', function(){

            $jq('.call_action_block').removeClass('active');

        });

        $jq('.call_action_content').mouseleave(function() {

            $jq('.call_action_block').removeClass('active');

        });

        //$jq('.call_action_contact').rotate(90);

    });





</script>

        /*** Popup leaving site ***/
        jQuery(document).ready(function() {
            jQuery('html').mouseleave(function() {
                var check_cookie = "";
                var qty = jQuery('#cartQty').attr('data-qty');
                if(!check_cookie && parseInt(qty) > 0) {
                    jQuery.fancybox({
                        'padding': '0',
                        'showCloseButton': false,
                        'type': 'inline',
                        'href': '#leaving-site',
                        'afterClose': function () {
                            /* La popup ne doit plus s'ouvrir pendant 7 jours */
                                                    }
                    });
                    jQuery(".fancybox-wrap").addClass("popUpLeavingWidth");
                    jQuery("#newsletter-popup").trigger('click');

                }
            });
        });
        /*** Popup leaving site account return ***/
        function saveMyCard(){
            jQuery.fancybox.close();
            jQuery('<input type="hidden" name="mouseLeaveMessage" value="mouseLeaveMessage">').insertAfter('#g-form-login .is_in_cart');
            jQuery('<input type="hidden" name="mouseLeaveMessage" value="mouseLeaveMessage">').insertAfter('#g-form-register-customer .is_in_cart');
            jQuery('#login-create_b').trigger('click');
        }
    </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
	function update_search()
	{
		var data	=	$jq("#formRecherche").serialize();
		
		$jq.ajax(
		{
			type:"GET",
			data:data,
			url:"https://www.leaderplant.com/ajax/reload_home_form.php",
			success:function(msg)
			{
				var res	=	msg.split("|");
				
				$jq("#formRecherche select[name='"+res[0]+"']").html(res[1]);
				$jq("#formRecherche select[name='"+res[0]+"']").removeAttr("disabled");	
			}
		});
	}
	function sendSearch()
	{
		var data	=	$jq("#formRecherche").serialize();
		
		data		=	str_replace("%25", "", data);
		var url		=	"https://www.leaderplant.com/recherche.html?"+data;
		
		document.location.href	=	url;	
	}
	
	function str_replace (search, replace, subject, count)
	{
	  var i = 0,
		j = 0,
		temp = '',
		repl = '',
		sl = 0,
		fl = 0,
		f = [].concat(search),
		r = [].concat(replace),
		s = subject,
		ra = Object.prototype.toString.call(r) === '[object Array]',
		sa = Object.prototype.toString.call(s) === '[object Array]';
	  s = [].concat(s);
	  if (count) {
		this.window[count] = 0;
	  }
	
	  for (i = 0, sl = s.length; i < sl; i++) {
		if (s[i] === '') {
		  continue;
		}
		for (j = 0, fl = f.length; j < fl; j++) {
		  temp = s[i] + '';
		  repl = ra ? (r[j] !== undefined ? r[j] : '') : r[0];
		  s[i] = (temp).split(f[j]).join(repl);
		  if (count && s[i] !== temp) {
			this.window[count] += (temp.length - s[i].length) / f[j].length;
		  }
		}
	  }
	  return sa ? s : s[0];
	}

	
</script><p></p>

<div class="inner siteweb">
<p>Bienvenue sur leaderplant.com, notre boutique de <a href="http://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-fleur-bambou-graminees.html">vente en ligne de plantes</a> de pépinières. Vous trouverez tout notre catalogue de plantes en direct de notre production. Notre pépinière française est spécialisée dans la production de jeunes plants d'arbres et d'arbustes, plantes de haie, bambous, graminées, vivaces, rosiers, plantes aromatiques, fruitiers et petits fruits. Retrouvez nos tops plantes pour votre jardin ou pour aménager votre balcon ou votre terrasse. <a href="https://www.leaderplant.com/plantes-de-haie/haie-de-brise-vue-vent/photinia.html" title="Vente photinia">Photinia</a>, <a href="https://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-photinia-red-1950.html" title="Photinia Red">Photinia Red</a>, <a href="	
https://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-cypres-cypres-de-leyland-1711.html">Cypres</a>, <a href="https://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-mertensia-maritima-2096.html">Mertensia</a>, <a href="https://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-aloe-vera-1631.html">Aloe</a>, <a href="https://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-bambou-noir-phyllostachys-nigra-1646.html">Phyllostachys Nigra</a>, <a href="https://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-griseline-1799.html">Griseline</a>, <a href="https://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-oranger-du-mexique-1681.html">Oranger du mexique</a>, <a href="https://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-eleagnus-ebbingei-chalef-1725.html">Eleagnus</a>, <a href="https://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-sagine-verte-scotch-moss-3789.html">Sagine</a>, <a href="https://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-jasmin-etoile-faux-jasmin-2290.html">Jasmin étoilé</a>, <a href="https://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-perovskia-lavande-d-afghanistan-1936.html">Perovskia</a>, <a href="https://www.leaderplant.com/acheter-kiwai-kiwi-de-siberie-autofertile-1624.html">Kiwai</a> et de nombreuses autres variétés à découvrir dans notre boutique en ligne. <script type="text/javascript">
        var formsToProtectOnPage = [];
        var currentForm;
        var currentValidationForm;
        var checkedForms = [];
        var hasFormsChanged = false;

        var onSubmit = function (token) {
            if (currentValidationForm && currentValidationForm.validator
                && currentValidationForm.validator.validate()
            ) {
                currentForm.querySelector('[name="amasty_invisible_token"]').setAttribute('value', token);
                if (typeof(recaptchaObject) !== 'undefined'
                    && 'post' !== currentForm.method
                ) {
                    recaptchaObject.submit();
                } else {
                    currentForm.submit();
                }
            } else {
                grecaptcha.reset();
            }
        };

        function checkForms() {
            formsToProtect = ["form[action*=\"customer\/account\/createpost\"]","form[action*=\"contacts\/index\/post\"]","form[id=newsletter-validate-detail]"];
            formsToProtect.forEach(function (item) {
                var continueWorking = true;
                formsSearchedBySelector = $$(item);
                checkedForms.forEach(function(element) {
                    if (element[0] == formsSearchedBySelector[0]) {
                        continueWorking = false;
                        return;
                    }
                })
                if (formsSearchedBySelector.length != 0 && continueWorking) {
                    checkedForms.push(formsSearchedBySelector);
                    formsSearchedBySelector.forEach(function (formToProtect) {
                        formsToProtectOnPage.push(formToProtect);
                        hasFormsChanged = true;
                    });
                }
            });
            if (hasFormsChanged) {
                for (var index in formsToProtectOnPage) {
                    if (formsToProtectOnPage.hasOwnProperty(index)) {
                        var formToProtectOnPage = formsToProtectOnPage[index];
                        if ('form' !== formToProtectOnPage.tagName.toLowerCase()) {
                            formToProtectOnPage = formToProtectOnPage.getElementsByTagName('form');
                            if (0 < formToProtectOnPage.length) {
                                formToProtectOnPage = formToProtectOnPage[0];
                            } else {
                                continue;
                            }
                        }

                        if (1 > formToProtectOnPage.getElementsByClassName('amasty_recaptcha').length) {
                            var recaptchaBlock = document.createElement('div');
                            recaptchaBlock.className = 'amasty_recaptcha';
                            formToProtectOnPage.appendChild(recaptchaBlock);
                        }

                        if (1 > formToProtectOnPage.getInputs(formToProtectOnPage, 'hidden', 'amasty_invisible_token').length) {
                            var tokenInput = document.createElement('input');
                            tokenInput.type = 'hidden';
                            tokenInput.name = 'amasty_invisible_token';
                            tokenInput.value = '';
                            formToProtectOnPage.appendChild(tokenInput);
                        }

                        formToProtectOnPage.onsubmit = function submitProtectedForm(event) {
                            currentForm = event.target;
                            currentValidationForm = new VarienForm(currentForm.id, false);
                            recaptchaBlock = currentForm.querySelector(".amasty_recaptcha");
                            if ('' == recaptchaBlock.innerHTML) {
                                recaptcha = grecaptcha.render(recaptchaBlock, {
                                    'sitekey': '6LcjXowUAAAAAEy2oQxzgWA4rgBSkVvdFXQmsdgE',
                                    'callback': onSubmit,
                                    'size': 'invisible',
                                    'theme': 'light',
                                    'badge': 'bottomright'
                                });
                            }
                            grecaptcha.reset(recaptcha);
                            grecaptcha.execute(recaptcha);
                            return false;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        document.observe("dom:loaded", function() {
            var formsCount = 0;
            setInterval(function () {
                var formLength = $$('form').length;
                if (formsCount != formLength) {
                    formsCount = formLength;
                    checkForms();
                }
            }, 1000);
        });
    </script>
